<script id="AUREL_R150_SUPPORT_RIGHT_PANEL_WHITE_JS">
(function(){
  function dark(el){
    function rgb(v){
      var m=String(v||"").match(/rgba?\((\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)(?:\s*,\s*([\d.]+))?\)/i);
      return m?{r:+m[1],g:+m[2],b:+m[3],a:m[4]===undefined?1:+m[4]}:null;
    }
    var cur=el;
    for(var i=0;i<8&&cur;i++,cur=cur.parentElement){
      var c=rgb(getComputedStyle(cur).backgroundColor);
      if(c&&c.a>.05) return (.2126*c.r+.7152*c.g+.0722*c.b)<125;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function apply(){
    var key=document.querySelector(".ai-key-panel");
    if(!key) return;
    var grids=[];
    var n=key.nextElementSibling;
    while(n&&grids.length<2){
      if(n.classList&&n.classList.contains("cols-equal")) grids.push(n);
      n=n.nextElementSibling;
    }
    document.querySelectorAll(".cols-equal").forEach(function(g){
      if(key.compareDocumentPosition(g)&Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING) grids.push(g);
    });
    var seen=new Set();
    grids.forEach(function(g){
      if(seen.has(g)) return; seen.add(g);
      var panels=Array.from(g.children).filter(function(x){return x.classList&&x.classList.contains("panel")});
      if(panels.length<2) return;
      var panel=panels[1];
      if(!dark(panel)) return;
      panel.style.setProperty("color","#ffffff","important");
      panel.querySelectorAll("*").forEach(function(el){
        var t=(el.textContent||"").trim();
        if(!t) return;
        var cls=String(el.className||"");
        if(/warn|status-chip/i.test(cls)){
          if(/Chờ duyệt|Đã duyệt|Đang xử lý/.test(t)){
            el.style.setProperty("color","#ffd36a","important");
          }
          return;
        }
        el.style.setProperty("color","#ffffff","important");
        el.style.setProperty("opacity","1","important");
      });
    });
  }
  var q=false;
  function schedule(){
    if(q)return;q=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){q=false;apply();});
  }
  if(document.readyState==="loading")document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",schedule,{once:true});else schedule();
  new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:["class","style"]});
  document.addEventListener("click",function(){setTimeout(apply,60)},true);
  setInterval(apply,1000);
})();
</script>

<script id="AUREL_R152_SUMMARY_THEME_COLOR_JS">
(function(){
  var PREFIX="Trong năm 2025, VPB";

  function norm(v){
    return String(v||"").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();
  }

  function isDarkMode(){
    return document.body.classList.contains("aurel-dark-theme");
  }

  function findSummaryBlock(){
    var walker=document.createTreeWalker(
      document.body,
      NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT,
      {
        acceptNode:function(node){
          var t=norm(node.nodeValue);
          return t.indexOf(PREFIX)===0
            ? NodeFilter.FILTER_ACCEPT
            : NodeFilter.FILTER_SKIP;
        }
      }
    );

    var node=walker.nextNode();
    if(!node) return null;

    var el=node.parentElement;
    if(!el) return null;

    var cur=el;
    for(var i=0;i<8 && cur && cur!==document.body;i++,cur=cur.parentElement){
      var t=norm(cur.textContent);
      var r=cur.getBoundingClientRect();
      if(
        t.indexOf(PREFIX)===0 &&
        t.length>220 &&
        t.length<5000 &&
        r.width>200 &&
        r.height>40
      ){
        return cur;
      }
    }
    return el;
  }

  function paint(){
    var el=findSummaryBlock();
    if(!el) return;

    document.querySelectorAll(".aurel-r152-summary-theme").forEach(function(x){
      if(x!==el) x.classList.remove("aurel-r152-summary-theme");
    });

    el.classList.add("aurel-r152-summary-theme");

    var color=isDarkMode() ? "#ffffff" : "#111111";

    function applyColor(x){
      x.style.setProperty("color",color,"important");
      x.style.setProperty("-webkit-text-fill-color",color,"important");
      x.style.setProperty("opacity","1","important");
      x.style.setProperty("filter","none","important");
    }

    applyColor(el);
    el.style.setProperty("font-weight","500","important");
    el.style.setProperty("line-height","1.72","important");

    el.querySelectorAll("*").forEach(applyColor);

    /* Remove any inherited dimming from immediate wrappers. */
    var p=el.parentElement;
    for(var i=0;i<3 && p && p!==document.body;i++,p=p.parentElement){
      p.style.setProperty("opacity","1","important");
      p.style.setProperty("filter","none","important");
    }
  }

  var queued=false;
  function schedule(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      paint();
    });
  }

  if(document.readyState==="loading"){
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",schedule,{once:true});
  }else{
    schedule();
  }

  new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(document.body,{
    childList:true,
    subtree:true,
    characterData:true,
    attributes:true,
    attributeFilter:["class","style"]
  });

  document.addEventListener("click",function(){setTimeout(paint,80)},true);
  setInterval(paint,800);
})();
</script>
</body></html>

  ];
  var SIDE=[
    'assets/aurel_sidebar_day_20261001.webp',
    'assets/aurel_sidebar_night_20261001.webp',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/8add7565-63fc-45a1-bc11-a74370f6e5a9-aurel_sidebar_sunrise_exact_20261001.png',
    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/2b9ccf62-48db-49be-b87a-6b58fc31c351-aurel_sidebar_night_t4_match_t3.webp?v=R97-REAL-CROP',

    'https://aurel-assets-20260929.floot.app/_cdn/static/b055cbff-17aa-4d5d-a249-9f1ed07d69fb-aurel_t8_danang_sidebar_night_20261001.png?v=R109-T8-DANANG-NIGHT'
  ];
  var HOLD=10000;
  var MOVE=900;
  var EASE='cubic-bezier(.22,.61,.36,1)';

  /* Một đồng hồ chung cho toàn bộ slideshow.
     Hero và sidebar không còn tự chạy timer riêng nên không thể lệch nhịp. */
  var instances=[];
  var globalIndex=0;
  var masterTimer=null;
  var animating=false;

  function preload(list){
    list.forEach(function(src){
      var im=new Image();
      im.decoding='async';
      im.src=src;
    });
  }

  function findInstance(el){
    for(var i=0;i<instances.length;i++){
      if(instances[i].el===el && instances[i].el.isConnected) return instances[i];
    }
    return null;
  }

  function mount(el,list,key){
    if(!el || !list || list.length<2) return null;

    var existing=findInstance(el);
    if(existing) return existing;

    var oldWrap=el.querySelector(':scope > .aurel-r78-slider-wrap');
    if(oldWrap) oldWrap.remove();

    var wrap=document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className='aurel-r78-slider-wrap';
    wrap.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

    var a=document.createElement('img');
    var b=document.createElement('img');
    a.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    b.className='aurel-r78-slider-img';
    a.alt=''; b.alt='';
    a.draggable=false; b.draggable=false;

    var idx=globalIndex%list.length;
    var nextIdx=(idx+1)%list.length;
    a.src=list[idx];
    b.src=list[nextIdx];
    a.style.transition='none';
    b.style.transition='none';
    a.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
    b.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';

    wrap.appendChild(a);
    wrap.appendChild(b);
    el.insertBefore(wrap,el.firstChild);

    var inst={
      el:el,
      list:list,
      key:key,
      wrap:wrap,
      current:a,
      next:b,
      index:idx
    };
    instances.push(inst);
    return inst;
  }

  function cleanup(){
    instances=instances.filter(function(inst){
      return inst.el && inst.el.isConnected && inst.wrap && inst.wrap.isConnected;
    });
  }

  function scheduleMaster(){
    if(masterTimer!==null) return;
    masterTimer=setTimeout(function(){
      masterTimer=null;
      advanceAll();
    },HOLD);
  }

  function advanceAll(){
    if(animating){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    cleanup();
    if(!instances.length){
      scheduleMaster();
      return;
    }

    animating=true;
    var ni=(globalIndex+1)%HERO.length;

    /* Chuẩn bị tất cả slider trong cùng một lượt JS. */
    instances.forEach(function(inst){
      var localNext=ni%inst.list.length;
      inst.next.src=inst.list[localNext];
      inst.current.style.transition='none';
      inst.next.style.transition='none';
      inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
      inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
    });

    /* Một cặp requestAnimationFrame chung: tất cả bắt đầu đúng cùng frame. */
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      requestAnimationFrame(function(){
        cleanup();
        instances.forEach(function(inst){
          inst.current.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.next.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(-100%,0,0)';
          inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
        });

        setTimeout(function(){
          cleanup();
          instances.forEach(function(inst){
            inst.current.style.transition='none';
            inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
            var old=inst.current;
            inst.current=inst.next;
            inst.next=old;
            inst.index=ni%inst.list.length;
          });
          globalIndex=ni;
          animating=false;
          scheduleMaster();
        },MOVE+40);
      });
    });
  }

  function run(){
    mount(document.querySelector('.sidebar'),SIDE,'side');
    mount(document.querySelector('.ref-hero'),HERO,'hero');
    scheduleMaster();
  }

  preload(HERO.concat(SIDE));

  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',run,{once:true});
  }else{
    run();
  }

  var root=document.getElementById('root')||document.body;
  var queued=false;
  new MutationObserver(function(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      cleanup();
      run();
    });
  }).observe(root,{chi
